Some observers have described Meloni's political positions as [far-right](221220.md),[^cite_note-259][^cite_note-Khrebtan-Hörhager_&_Pyatovskaya_2022-260] and have highlighted her campaign appearances with [Rachele Mussolini](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rachele_Mussolini_\(politician\)).[^cite_note-FemmeFascista-261] In August 2018, Friedel Taube wrote in *[Deutsche Welle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deutsche_Welle)* that "Giorgia Meloni has a long history in far-right politics".[^cite_note-262] In a July 2022 interview with Nicholas Farrell of *[The Spectator](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Spectator)*, Meloni rejected descriptions of her politics as far right, calling it a [smear campaign](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Smear_campaign) by her opponents and cited British [conservative](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conservative) philosopher [Roger Scruton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roger_Scruton) as one of her influences.[^cite_note-Farrell-2022-263] She has described herself as a mainstream conservative.[^cite_note-264][^cite_note-265] Additionally, Meloni has been described as [hard right](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Radical_right_\(Europe\)),[^cite_note-266] [right-wing populist](8354382.md),[^cite_note-267][^cite_note-268] and [nationalist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nationalist).[^cite_note-269][^cite_note-270][^cite_note-271]

Meloni has been described as being close to [Viktor Orbán](750582.md), the former [prime minister of Hungary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prime_minister_of_Hungary) and leader of [Fidesz](404647.md),[^cite_note-272][^cite_note-273] and [Rishi Sunak](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rishi_Sunak), the former [British prime minister](24150.md) and [Conservatives](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conservative_Party_\(UK\)) leader.[^cite_note-274][^cite_note-275] She has also been linked with the [Vox](41928488.md) political party in Spain,[^cite_note-276] representatives of the [Law and Justice](520309.md) party in Poland,[^cite_note-277][^cite_note-278] and the [Republican Party](32070.md) in the United States.[^cite_note-279][^cite_note-280][^cite_note-281] Meloni has self-described her political party, [Brothers of Italy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brothers_of_Italy) (FdI), as a mainstream conservative party.[^cite_note-282][^cite_note-Amante_&_Balmer_2022-97][^cite_note-283] She is in favour of [presidentialism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Presidentialism) and supports changes to the [Constitution of Italy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Constitution_of_Italy).[^cite_note-Pietromarchi_2022-3]

In June 2024, Meloni criticised the [EU ban on the sale of new petrol and diesel cars from 2035](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Green_Deal) that would "condemn \[Europe] to new strategic dependencies, such as China's electric \[vehicles]". According to Meloni: "Reducing polluting emissions is the path we want to follow, but with common sense."[^cite_note-284]

### Social issues



Meloni speaking at a [Vox](41928488.md) rally in [Málaga](184763.md), Spain

Meloni opposes abortion, [euthanasia](9587.md), and laws that recognise [same-sex marriage](92656.md), and describes herself as "pro-family".[^cite_note-285][^cite_note-Torrisi_2022-286][^cite_note-287] She has said she "wouldn't change" the [abortion law in Italy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abortion_law_in_Italy) but wanted to apply more fully the part of the law "about prevention", such as permitting doctors to refuse to carry them out ([conscientious objection to abortion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conscientious_objection_to_abortion)).[^cite_note-288][^cite_note-289] She also stated that the [recognition of same-sex unions in Italy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Recognition_of_same-sex_unions_in_Italy) is good enough,[^cite_note-290][^cite_note-Bollinger_2022-291] and she said it was something she would not change;[^cite_note-292] in 2016, while she said she would respect the law if elected [mayor of Rome](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mayor_of_Rome), she had supported a [referendum](51582.md) to abrogate the civil union law.[^cite_note-293][^cite_note-294] At a rally at the [Piazza del Popolo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Piazza_del_Popolo) in October 2019, she spoke against [same-sex parenting](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Same-sex_parenting); her speech became viral on Italian social media platforms.[^cite_note-295] During a February 2016 interview to *[Le Iene](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Le_Iene)*, an Italian television show, she had also said that she would "rather not have a gay child".[^cite_note-296][^cite_note-Carlo_2022-297][^cite_note-298]

Meloni has opposed the [1993 Mancino law](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1993_Mancino_law), a [hate speech](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hate_speech) law.[^cite_note-Brezar_2022-15] She is opposed to the [DDL Zan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/DDL_Zan), an [anti-homophobia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-homophobia) law that would expand the Mancino law to cover LGBT discrimination, declaring in 2020 that "there is no homophobia" in Italy.[^cite_note-299] She is also opposed to [surrogacy](1514981.md), which is pejoratively known in Italian as *utero in affitto* ('uterus renting'),[^cite_note-300][^cite_note-301] and she has pushed in Parliament for a law to make it a "universal crime"; her efforts have been endorsed by the [Catholic Church](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catholic_Church_in_Italy) and by [Pope Francis](1687680.md) himself.[^cite_note-302][^cite_note-Bollinger_2022-291][^cite_note-303]

Meloni is supportive of the [anti-gender movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-gender_movement), based on [Catholic theology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catholic_theology) in the 1990s that condemns [gender studies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gender_studies), and she is sceptical of what she calls "gender ideology";[^cite_note-304][^cite_note-305] she says it is being taught in schools,[^cite_note-306][^cite_note-307][^cite_note-308] and that it attacks female identity and motherhood.[^cite_note-309] She is supportive of changing the Constitution of Italy to make it illegal for same-sex couples to adopt children.[^cite_note-310] In March 2018, she criticised [The Walt Disney Company](37398.md) for the decision to represent a gay couple in the musical fantasy film *[Frozen II](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Frozen_II)*. On Facebook, she wrote: "Enough! We are sick of it! Take your hands off the children."[^cite_note-311][^cite_note-312][^cite_note-313]

#### Feminism



Meloni at the [European Council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Council) in March 2023

In her 2011 book *We Believe*, Meloni wrote: "I am a right-wing woman, and I proudly support women's issues. In recent years we have had to suffer contempt and racism by feminists. ... Perhaps as far as feminism is conceived in this way, it is more a question of ideology than of gender and substance."[^cite_note-314] She is opposed to pink quotas and has denied being anti-women as accused by some critics.[^cite_note-315][^cite_note-316][^cite_note-317]

The possibility of Meloni becoming the first woman to become [Prime Minister of Italy](273741.md) had been widely discussed both prior to and after the [2022 Italian general election](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_Italian_general_election). Some women did not see this as a victory due to her political positions, while others saw it at least partly in a positive light, and a few others called her a feminist despite Meloni's rejection of the label.[^cite_note-318] Prior to the election, former U.S. secretary of state [Hillary Clinton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hillary_Clinton) commented: "The election of the first woman prime minister in a country always represents a break with the past, and that is certainly a good thing."[^cite_note-319][^cite_note-320]

This prompted a response from some critics and observers, including historians [Ruth Ben-Ghiat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ruth_Ben-Ghiat) and David Broder. Ben-Ghiat wrote: "Meloni would also represent continuity with Italy's darkest episode."[^cite_note-Ben-Ghiat_2022-321] For her part, Meloni declared herself ready to govern and criticised feminists.[^cite_note-322] In August 2025, [Mihail Neamțu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mihail_Neam%C8%9Bu) wrote: "Unlike [Thatcher](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Margaret_Thatcher)'s emphasis on economic freedoms, and unlike modern feminism's insistence on rights and recognition, Meloni grounds her conservatism in a deeper sense of duty \[...] a vision of womanhood that is not a repudiation of tradition, but its vindication."[^cite_note-323] In November 2025, Meloni supported the passing of the [law against femicide](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Femicide_in_Italy).[^cite_note-324]

### Immigration and multiculturalism



Meloni with [Mark Rutte](4321056.md), [Ursula von der Leyen](2991932.md), and [Kais Saied](61361599.md) signing Tunisian aid legislation and an agreement against [illegal immigration](7124412.md)



Meloni with Ethiopian prime minister [Abiy Ahmed](56624029.md) and other leaders at the International Conference on Development and Migration in Rome, 23 July 2023

Meloni has criticised Italy's approach towards [illegal immigrants](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Illegal_immigrants), calling for a [zero-tolerance](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zero-tolerance) policy. She wants to block illegal immigrants from reaching Italian ports,[^cite_note-Nielsen_2022-91] and boost the birth rate of Italian nationals to ease the need for migrant labour.[^cite_note-325] She is opposed to [birthright citizenship](54035.md) proposals, which would give citizenship including education rights to foreigners born and living in Italy.[^cite_note-Farrell_2022-326]

Amid the 2022 escalation of the [Russo-Ukrainian War](42085878.md), Meloni said she supported giving refugee status to those coming from a war-shaken country but not to other [asylum seekers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Asylum_seekers). She said: "It's time to call things by their name, to give refugee status to those fleeing war, women, and children, perhaps doing the opposite with those who aren't refugees."[^cite_note-327]

In August 2022, she reposted a [pixelated](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pixelation) video on Twitter that shows a woman being raped by an asylum seeker.[^cite_note-328] The victim of the violence decried the publication of the video and said she was recognised by the video posted.[^cite_note-329] After receiving backlash, Meloni defended herself by accusing other politicians of not having condemned the rape itself.[^cite_note-330]

Meloni has blamed [neo-colonialism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neo-colonialism) for Africa's underdevelopment and the [2015 European migrant crisis](46415102.md), and she said she favours co-operation over what she termed [France's neocolonialism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neocolonialism#Fran%C3%A7afrique).[^cite_note-331] She has been considered as opposed to the reception of illegal immigrants,[^cite_note-332][^cite_note-333][^cite_note-334] as well as to [multiculturalism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multiculturalism),[^cite_note-335][^cite_note-336] and she has been described as [xenophobic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Xenophobic),[^cite_note-337][^cite_note-338] as well as [Islamophobic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islamophobic),[^cite_note-Khrebtan-Hörhager_&_Pyatovskaya_2022-260][^cite_note-339][^cite_note-340] by some critics.

In 2018, she said she would welcome Venezuelans, saying they are Christians and often of Italian origin.[^cite_note-341] She has often criticised [George Soros](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Soros) and what she terms *[globalists](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Globalist_\(epithet\))*, at times reflecting the views of [Soros conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Soros_conspiracy_theories),[^cite_note-342] once saying: "When you are a slave, you act in Soros's interests."[^cite_note-343] She has endorsed the [Great Replacement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Great_Replacement), a [white nationalist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/White_nationalist) conspiracy theory.[^cite_note-344]

She believes there is a planned mass migration from Africa to Europe for the purpose of replacing and eliminating Italians, an antisemitic, far-right conspiracy theory known as the [Kalergi Plan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kalergi_Plan).[^cite_note-345][^cite_note-346][^cite_note-347] She has described pro-immigration policies as part of an alleged left-wing conspiracy to "replace Italians with immigrants". In January 2017, she called [immigration to Italy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Immigration_to_Italy) "ethnic substitution".[^cite_note-Farrell_2022-326]

Meloni complained about the danger of [ethnic substitution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Great_Replacement) in her 2019 book about the [Nigerian mafia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nigerian_mafia),[^cite_note-Bottura-348][^cite_note-Pucciarelli-349] co-written with [Alessandro Meluzzi](https://it.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alessandro%20Meluzzi), [anti-vaccine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-vaccine_activism) psychiatrist, founder of the "Anti-Islamisation Party" and at the time [primate](24672.md) of a schismatic Italian Orthodox Church.[^cite_note-350][^cite_note-351][^cite_note-352] Along with other [white supremacist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/White_supremacist) stereotypes, the book argues that a project is underway to "change the European ethnicity and create Eurafrica", that the Nigerian mafia is the product of "local cultures that practice ritual murder and cannibalism", and that "the corpses of white people are very appreciated" by the [Yoruba](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yoruba_people), who are said to be engaged in the trade of human flesh and organs.[^cite_note-Bottura-348][^cite_note-Pucciarelli-349]

In 2023, amidst an unprecedented migration crisis, she asserted that Europe and Italy need immigration and that only illegal immigration must be fought in favour of legal immigration.[^cite_note-353][^cite_note-354] Meloni wanted to make a deal with Tunisian president [Kais Saied](61361599.md) with a focus on stopping illegal immigration from Tunisia to Europe. In September 2023, more than 120 boats carrying around 7,000 migrants from Africa arrived on the Italian island of [Lampedusa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lampedusa) within 24 hours, increasing the volume handled by the local migration reception centre by 15 times and leading to the migrants outnumbering the island's native population.[^cite_note-355] Meloni declared that she wrote to the European Commission president Ursula von der Leyen "to ask her to come with me to Lampedusa to personally realise the gravity of the situation we face, and to immediately accelerate the implementation of the agreement with Tunisia by transferring the agreed resources".[^cite_note-356]

In November 2023, Giorgia Meloni and Albanian Prime Minister [Edi Rama](277805.md) signed an immigration agreement that allowed Italy, for at least five years, to make use of specified locations in Albania to establish two migrant management facilities, funded and placed under Italian jurisdiction. Building work started in March 2024, with the initial structures finished later that year. The port of [Shëngjin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sh%C3%ABngjin) hosts an arrival facility, and an asylum complex operates on a nearby military base close to the village of [Gjadër](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gjad%C3%ABr).[^cite_note-migops-357]

The detention centre in Gjadër has been criticised by politicians and human rights observers for its lack of transparency, prison-like conditions, and serious risks to detainees' mental health, including reports of frequent self-harm, suicide attempts, and inadequate access to legal and medical safeguards. The asylum centre has room for 36,000 migrants, but by October 2025, only 256 have been detained there. In most cases, judicial decisions resulted in their return to Italy.[^cite_note-migops-357]

The Meloni government issued over 450,000 permits to migrants between 2023 and 2025. In July 2025, it was reported that the government will issue nearly half a million or 500,000, work visas for non-EU nationals from 2026 to 2028.[^cite_note-358][^cite_note-359][^cite_note-360] The quotas are reserved for selected countries, primarily located in [Asia](689.md), [Africa](5334607.md), and the [Western Balkans](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Western_Balkans).[^cite_note-361]

In 2025, the Meloni government enacted a decree which significantly restricted Italian citizenship by descent, affecting millions of [Italian descendants](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_diaspora). Citizenship claims are now restricted to applicants who have a parent or grandparent born in Italy who held Italian citizenship at birth. Great-grandchildren and more distant descendants no longer qualify automatically.[^cite_note-362][^cite_note-363]

In July 2026, following the [Morocco–Spain border incident](83824713.md), Meloni called for [Spain](26667.md) to be suspended from the EU's visa-free [Schengen Area](16565139.md). Writing on [X](9988187.md), she said that "uncontrolled illegal immigration poses a real threat to the security of Europe's borders."[^cite_note-PoliticoEU-364] Domestically, her position was supported by members of her governing coalition, including Deputy Prime Minister [Matteo Salvini](1767165.md).[^cite_note-PoliticoEU-364] Spanish Prime Minister [Pedro Sánchez](43297512.md) rejected Meloni's criticism and argued on X that Italy had received significantly more migrant arrivals than Spain since 2021, citing approximately 478,000 arrivals in Italy compared with 234,000 in Spain.[^cite_note-365]

### Foreign issues



Meloni with French president [Emmanuel Macron](43671127.md) in 2022

Meloni followed the PdL party line in favour of the [2011 military intervention in Libya](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_military_intervention_in_Libya); however, in 2019, she criticised the French rationale for the intervention, stating it was because of [Muammar Gaddafi](53029.md)'s opposition to the [CFA franc](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CFA_franc).[^cite_note-366][^cite_note-367] She was critical of Italian relations with [Saudi Arabia](349303.md) and [Qatar](8238258.md), stating that these countries "systematically and deliberately spread fundamentalist theories that are the main causes of the growth of [Islamic fundamentalism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islamic_fundamentalism)".[^cite_note-368]

She opposed the decision to host the [Supercoppa Italiana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Supercoppa_Italiana) final in Saudi Arabia, and stated that Italy should actively raise the issue of [human rights in Saudi Arabia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Human_rights_in_Saudi_Arabia).[^cite_note-369] However, upon taking office, Meloni reversed her position, with her government stating it was "keen to maintain the excellent relationship with Saudi Arabia" yet still calling for a "firm reaction" against Qatar to which several Italians were accused of involvement in [Qatargate](72479300.md).[^cite_note-370]

In 2021, Meloni stated her party "denounced the authoritarian, Islamist direction [Erdogan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Erdogan)'s Turkey has taken for years and asked the EU to withdraw Ankara's status as a candidate country",[^cite_note-371] but upon taking office, pursued closer ties with the Turkish government, due to [Italy's interests in Libya](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italy%E2%80%93Libya_relations), cooperation in stopping [illegal immigration](7124412.md), shared nationalist values and common disagreement with French foreign policy.[^cite_note-372] Meloni advocated for the expulsion of the Indian ambassador to Italy as a result of the [Enrica Lexie case](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Enrica_Lexie_case),[^cite_note-373] and she urged [Alessandro Del Piero](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alessandro_Del_Piero) to refuse to play in the [Indian Super League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indian_Super_League) until the detained Italian marines were returned.[^cite_note-374] Following the [Asia Bibi blasphemy case](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Asia_Bibi_blasphemy_case), Meloni criticised what she called the "silence of the West" and advocated a stronger stance by the international community against [human rights violations in Pakistan](4513212.md).[^cite_note-375]

Meloni with the Ukrainian president Volodymyr Zelenskyy in 2022

Meloni with Ukrainian president [Volodymyr Zelenskyy](59210523.md) in 2023

Prior to the [2022 Russian invasion of Ukraine](79400268.md), she was in favour of better relations with Russia and supported lifting [sanctions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_sanctions_during_the_Russo-Ukrainian_War) on Russia in 2014.[^cite_note-376] In 2018, she congratulated [Vladimir Putin](32817.md) for his re-election as president.[^cite_note-377] In 2021, she wrote that [Russia under Putin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russia_under_Putin) defends European values and Christian identity.[^cite_note-378] Since the 2022 invasion, she has pledged to send arms to Ukraine[^cite_note-379] and moved towards [Atlanticism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Atlanticism).[^cite_note-380][^cite_note-381][^cite_note-382] In September 2022, she said that Russia's [annexation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_annexation_of_Donetsk,_Kherson,_Luhansk_and_Zaporizhzhia_oblasts) of four partially occupied provinces in southeastern Ukraine has "no legal and political value".[^cite_note-383]

She is supportive of [NATO](21133.md),[^cite_note-384] although she maintains [Eurosceptic](10037.md) views towards the EU,[^cite_note-385][^cite_note-386][^cite_note-387] having also previously advocated a withdrawal from the [eurozone](184391.md).[^cite_note-388][^cite_note-389] She rejects the Eurosceptic label, favouring the [Eurorealism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eurorealism) of a confederal Europe of sovereign nations.[^cite_note-390] Following the start of Trump's second term, Meloni has been noted for her efforts to preserve the transatlantic alliance leveraging her relationship with Trump and credited for orchestrating a rapprochement between Trump and Ukrainian president Zelensky at the 2025 [funeral of Pope Francis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Death_and_funeral_of_Pope_Francis).[^cite_note-391][^cite_note-392][^cite_note-393][^cite_note-394]

Giorgia Meloni and Luiz Inácio Lula da Silva in 2023

Meloni with Brazilian president [Luiz Inácio Lula da Silva](99328.md) in 2023

A critic of [China](5405.md), Meloni is a supporter of closer ties between Italy and [Taiwan](25734.md).[^cite_note-395] She is a controversial figure in Croatia due to her [Italian irredentist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_irredentist) statements in which she claimed [Dalmatia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dalmatia) and [Istria](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Istria), and for being opposed to Croatian entry into the EU due to the unresolved dispute concerning properties of [exiled Italians](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Istrian%E2%80%93Dalmatian_exodus) after [World War II](32927.md) from these two Croatian regions.[^cite_note-396][^cite_note-397]

In 2018, she said that [Iran](14653.md) and Lebanese militant group [Hezbollah](13919.md) were protectors of [Syrian Christians](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christianity_in_Syria) in the [Syrian civil war](30741795.md).[^cite_note-398] She condemned "another massacre of children in Gaza" during the [2014 Gaza War](43243199.md) between [Israel](9282173.md) and the Palestinian militant group [Hamas](13913.md),[^cite_note-399] but later changed her stance and became a supporter of closer relations with Israel.[^cite_note-400] Unlike [Matteo Salvini](1767165.md), she is opposed to moving the Italian embassy to Israel from Tel Aviv to Jerusalem,[^cite_note-401] opposed the [Rafah offensive](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rafah_offensive) during the ongoing [Gaza war](74998519.md), and is supportive of a [two-state solution](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Two-state_solution).[^cite_note-402] After the [2023 Israeli invasion of Gaza](74998519.md), she reversed her pro-Israel stance and imposed a full arms embargo on Israel.[^cite_note-403]

Meloni has not apologised to African nations for wrongs committed during the [Italian colonial period](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_Empire),[^cite_note-404] but is vocally critical of the legacy of the [French colonial empire](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/French_colonial_empire) in Africa, arguing that France continues to exploit its former colonies through the [CFA franc](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CFA_franc).[^cite_note-405]

Though previously known to be on good terms with U.S. President [Donald Trump](4848272.md), which even included her being the only European leader to attend his [second inauguration](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Second_inauguration_of_Donald_Trump) in January 2025, Meloni and Trump came into conflict in April 2026 due to their disputes over both Trump's criticism of [Pope Leo XIV](54520372.md), which included him posting on social media an AI image of him as a Jesus-like figure, and his handling of the [2026 Iran War](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Iran_War), with Trump lashing out at Meloni and, among other things, calling her "much different than I thought."[^cite_note-406][^cite_note-407][^cite_note-408][^cite_note-409]

### Energy policy

In March 2026, Meloni called for an urgent suspension of the [EU Emissions Trading System](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Union_Emissions_Trading_System) (ETS, which sets a limit on greenhouse gas emissions from key sectors, incentivising reductions through carbon pricing and trading of emission allowances), for electricity production, citing the need to lower soaring energy prices triggered by the crisis in the Middle East.[^cite_note-410]

On 18 March 2026, Meloni and nine other EU member state leaders requested a review of the EU Emissions Trading System (ETS) in a joint letter to European authorities. In the letter, the heads of state and government called for an extension of free [emissions allowances](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/EU_Allowance) beyond 2034, along with a more gradual phase-out of free quotas starting in 2028, citing the need to support European industry.[^cite_note-411]

Her stance on global warming has been called (by *Politico*) "a delicate tightrope", as her movement has traditionally criticised concern over global warming as "elitist and against the people", and she herself has denounced "ultra-ecological fanaticism" as a threat to the economy. At the same time extreme heat during the summer of 2022 is estimated to have killed 18,000 Italians,[^cite_note-Giordano_Denial_Politico-2023-412] and a February March 2023 Eurobarometer poll found 75% of Italians thought her government "hasn’t done enough" to counter climate-related phenomena.[^cite_note-Bonini-EUNews-2025-413]

### COVID-19 pandemic and vaccines

Meloni has exhibited support for [vaccine hesitancy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vaccine_hesitancy), such as not vaccinating her daughter during the [COVID-19 pandemic in Italy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/COVID-19_pandemic_in_Italy) because "it's not a religion".[^cite_note-414][^cite_note-415][^cite_note-416] She has been criticised due to her statements on [vaccines](32653.md) and [COVID-19](63030231.md), stating the probability of someone aged 0–19 dying from COVID-19 was the same as being struck by lightning.[^cite_note-417][^cite_note-418] After her party won the [2022 Italian general election](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_Italian_general_election), they pledged to review the positions taken by the Italian government during the [COVID-19 pandemic](62750956.md) and end the [COVID-19 vaccine mandate](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/COVID-19_vaccine_mandate) in place for health care workers.[^cite_note-419]

### Relationship with fascism

During her political career, Meloni has made statements that generated controversy.[^cite_note-420] In an interview to the French newscast *[Soir 3](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Soir_3)* when she was 19,[^cite_note-421] she praised Italian dictator [Benito Mussolini](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Benito_Mussolini) as "a good politician, in that everything he did, he did for Italy",[^cite_note-422][^cite_note-423][^cite_note-424][^cite_note-425] and as the best politician of the last 50 years.[^cite_note-426] In January 2020, there was some controversy after Meloni and the *[comune](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Comune)* ('municipality') of Verona supported naming a street after [Giorgio Almirante](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Giorgio_Almirante); Meloni and the *comune* also supported giving [Liliana Segre](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Liliana_Segre), a [Holocaust survivor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Holocaust_survivor) and [senator for life](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Senator_for_life), honorary citizenship. Segre said that she and Almirante were incompatible and the *comune* had to make a choice.[^cite_note-427][^cite_note-428]

In May 2020, Meloni praised Almirante as a "great politician", as well as "a patriot".[^cite_note-429][^cite_note-430][^cite_note-431] He was the co-founder of the [Italian Social Movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_Social_Movement) (MSI), who had a long post-war political career until retiring in 1987. During [World War II](32927.md), he was a [wartime collaborator](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wartime_collaborator) as a civil minister of the [Italian Social Republic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_Social_Republic) (RSI), a Nazi [puppet state](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Puppet_state),[^cite_note-432] as well as editor-in-chief of the antisemitic and racist magazine *[La Difesa della Razza](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_Difesa_della_Razza)*, which published the "[Manifesto of Race](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Manifesto_of_Race)" in 1938.[^cite_note-Carlo_2022-297][^cite_note-433][^cite_note-434]

As a minister in 2009, Meloni visited [Yad Vashem](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yad_Vashem) in Israel.[^cite_note-Sergi_2022-76] She has said as FdI party leader that her party "handed fascism over to history for decades now" and it "unambiguously condemns the suppression of democracy and the ignominious [anti-Jewish laws](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_racial_laws)".[^cite_note-Pietromarchi_2022-3]

In November 2018, Meloni declared that the celebration of the [Liberation Day](39216075.md), also known as the Anniversary of Italy's Liberation from Nazi-Fascism on 25 April, and *[Festa della Repubblica](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Festa_della_Repubblica)*, which celebrates the birth of the Italian Republic on 2 June, should be substituted with the [National Unity and Armed Forces Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Unity_and_Armed_Forces_Day) on 4 November, which commemorates Italy's victory in [World War I](4764461.md). She said that Liberation Day and *Festa della Repubblica* are "two controversial celebrations".[^cite_note-435] Meloni has tried to distance herself from her ties to Roberto Jonghi Lavarini, a Milanese politician and entrepreneur known as the "Black Baron".[^cite_note-436]



Meloni with the tricolour flame in the background, 2014

After the formation of FdI in 2012, she decided to add the tricolour flame to the party flag, a [neo-fascist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neo-fascist) symbol associated with the MSI, which derived its name and ideals from the RSI.[^cite_note-437] The tricolour flame is said to represent Mussolini's remains, where a flame is always burning on his tomb in [Predappio](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Predappio).[^cite_note-438] Heading into the 2022 general election, Segre told *Pagine Ebraiche* that Meloni should remove the tricolour flame from the party's logo. FdI's co-founder [Ignazio La Russa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ignazio_La_Russa) rejected this view,[^cite_note-Segre_12_August-85] and Meloni ignored the request, keeping the tricolour flame.[^cite_note-439]

Observers, including historians [Ruth Ben-Ghiat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ruth_Ben-Ghiat), David Broder, and [Laurence Bertrand Dorléac](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Laurence_Bertrand_Dorl%C3%A9ac),[^cite_note-Broder_2022-81][^cite_note-440] said that Meloni and FdI have been ambiguous about their fascist past,[^cite_note-441] at times rejecting it and at other times minimising it, and that this has helped to rebrand both herself and her party.[^cite_note-442] Responding to the 2021 *[Fanpage.it](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fanpage.it)* report, she minimised the investigation and refused to remove openly neo-fascist members of FdI.[^cite_note-443]

In December 2021, FdI's Alfredo Catapano and Luigi Rispoli were among former MSI members who did a [Roman salute](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roman_salute), which was condemned by the [ANPI](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ANPI). Rispoli told *Fanpage.it*: "I believe in the [New Right](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_Right) and in the efforts Giorgia Meloni is making in Brothers of Italy. It makes me wonder, frankly, this clamour."[^cite_note-444] Shortly before the 2022 general election, she sacked a member that openly praised [Adolf Hitler](2731583.md).[^cite_note-BBC_2022-89] FdI had also distanced itself from the [Ascoli Piceno](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ascoli_Piceno) party section after it celebrated the anniversary of the [March on Rome](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/March_on_Rome) in 2019.[^cite_note-445]

On 25 October 2022, on the occasion of the vote of confidence of the Parliament in the government, Meloni in her speech before the deputies said: "Freedom and democracy are the distinctive elements of contemporary European civilisation in which I have always recognised myself. And therefore, despite the instrumental argument of my opponents, I have never had sympathy for undemocratic regimes. For any regime, including fascism. Exactly as I have always considered the racial laws of 1938 the lowest point in Italian history, a shame that will mark our people forever."[^cite_note-446][^cite_note-447][^cite_note-448]

In July 2024, Meloni was awarded damages in a defamation lawsuit against journalist Giulia Cortese, who in October 2021 had posted a photo of Meloni on Twitter, now X, which was altered to show Mussolini in the background. Cortese later mocked Meloni's height, stating, "You don't scare me, Giorgia Meloni. After all, you're only 1.2 metres (4 ft) tall. I can't even see you."[^cite_note-449] Cortese was not charged for posting the photo comparing Meloni to Mussolini, but was fined for comments about Meloni's height that were deemed "body shaming".[^cite_note-450] Cortese criticised the judgment in a post on X, stating, "Italy's government has a serious problem with freedom of expression and journalistic dissent."[^cite_note-451]

## Personal life



Meloni receives a [birthday cake](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Birthday_cake) for her forty-ninth birthday, during her official visit to Japan in January 2026.

In 2015, Meloni began a relationship with [Andrea Giambruno](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andrea_Giambruno), a journalist working for [Mediaset](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mediaset) TV channels.[^cite_note-452] The couple have a daughter, Ginevra, born in September 2016.[^cite_note-453][^cite_note-454] On 20 October 2023, she announced her split with Giambruno, following his off-air statements transmitted by the television program *[Striscia la notizia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Striscia_la_notizia)* that were described as "sexist" and "chauvinist", which included propositioning a female colleague for a [threesome](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Threesome).[^cite_note-455][^cite_note-456][^cite_note-457] Meloni added that "all those who hoped to weaken \[her] by attacking \[her] personal life should know that however much the drop of water tries to dig out the stone, the stone remains stone and the drop is only water".[^cite_note-458] She defended herself, not being married to her child's father.[^cite_note-459]

Meloni is a [Catholic](606848.md) and has used her religious identity in part to help build her national brand. In a 2019 speech to a rally in Rome, she said: "I am Giorgia. I am a woman, I am a mother, I am Italian, I am Christian."[^cite_note-Pietromarchi_2022-3][^cite_note-460][^cite_note-461]

In September 2022, Meloni reportedly continued to embrace the trinomial "*[Dio, patria, famiglia](https://it.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dio,%20patria,%20famiglia)*" ('God, fatherland, family').[^cite_note-462] She has said she resents being linked to Italy's fascist past.

In addition to her native Italian, Meloni speaks English, Spanish, and French.[^cite_note-463]

Meloni is a fan of fantasy, particularly [J. R. R. Tolkien](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/J._R._R._Tolkien)'s *[The Lord of the Rings](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Lord_of_the_Rings)*. As a youth activist with the [Italian Social Movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian_Social_Movement) (MSI), she attended the 1993 revival of the [Camp Hobbit](https://it.wikipedia.org/wiki/Campi%20Hobbit#I_successivi_Campi_Hobbit) festival ("Hobbit 93") and sang along with the folk band [Compagnia dell'Anello](https://it.wikipedia.org/wiki/Compagnia%20dell'Anello%20\(gruppo%20musicale\)), named after *[The Fellowship of the Ring](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Fellowship_of_the_Ring)* (1954).[^cite_note-Seibt2022-464][^cite_note-465] Later, she named her political conference [Atreyu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Atreyu_\(The_Neverending_Story\)), after the hero of the novel *[The Neverending Story](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Neverending_Story)* (1979).[^cite_note-466] Meloni told *[The New York Times](30680.md)*: "I think that Tolkien could say better than we can what conservatives believe in."[^cite_note-Seibt2022-464]

In November 2023, Meloni inaugurated a major exhibition on J. R. R. Tolkien at the National Gallery of Modern and Contemporary Art in Rome to mark the 50th anniversary of the author's death.[^cite_note-467] Apart from Tolkien, she is fond of British conservative philosopher [Roger Scruton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roger_Scruton) and has said: "If I were British I would be a [Tory](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tory)."[^cite_note-468]

## Honours and recognition

*[Forbes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Forbes)* ranked Meloni as the seventh [most powerful woman in the world](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Forbes_list_of_the_World's_100_Most_Powerful_Women) in 2022[^cite_note-469] and placed her third in 2023 and 2024.[^cite_note-470][^cite_note-471] In 2024 she was named one of the [100 most influential people in the world](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_100) by *[Time](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_\(magazine\))* magazine.[^cite_note-472][^cite_note-473] That same year, Meloni also received the [Atlantic Council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Atlantic_Council)'s [Global Citizen Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Global_Citizen_Awards).[^cite_note-474][^cite_note-475] *[Politico Europe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Politico_Europe)* ranked her as Europe's Most Powerful Person in their Class of 2025.[^cite_note-476]

### Foreign honours

-  [Finland](10577.md): Grand Cross of the [Order of the White Rose of Finland](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Order_of_the_White_Rose_of_Finland) (2023)

  [^cite_note-477]

-  [Spain](26667.md): Dame Grand Cross of the [Order of Isabella the Catholic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Order_of_Isabella_the_Catholic) (2024)

  [^cite_note-478]

-  [Ukraine](31750.md): Member of the [Order of Liberty](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Order_of_Liberty_\(Ukraine\)) (2024)

  [^cite_note-479]

-  [IOC](15147.md): Gold [Olympic Order](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Olympic_Order) (2026)

  [^cite_note-480]
